Procedure:
1. Start with P (nonnegative length n).
2. Compute C = conv(P,P) length 2n-1.
3. Modify C to be flatter and feasible: e.g., C_new = median/quantile cap, ensure nonnegative smooth, maybe adjust sum to match S^2? But S will change.
4. Recover P_new from C_new using formal square root: recursively compute a0..a_{n-1} from first n coefficients C_new[0:n], assuming c0>0. This gives exactly a polynomial P_new whose first n square coefficients equal C_new[0:n]. Then the remaining tail coefficients are determined; if they exceed M, loop. This is like taking square root in polynomial ring. It preserves nonnegativity only if recursion positive; clip if negative.

This is very appealing. We can choose C_new to minimize max. Let's analyze recursion and sign. Let C_new[0]=m0. a0=√m0. For k=1..n-1:
a_k = (C_k - sum_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i C? Actually C_k = 2a0 a_k + ∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i} (with i=k? no, for i=k term a_k a0 included in first; diagonal if k even? Let's derive full:
C_k = ∑_{i=0}^k a_i a_{k-i}.
For k>=1, terms i=0 and i=k: a0 a_k + a_k a0 =2a0 a_k.
Terms i=1..k-1: if i ≠ k-i, both appear separately in sum; if i=k/2 (k even), term a_i^2 appears once (because i=k-i). If we compute H_k=∑_{i=1}^{k-1} a_i a_{k-i}, includes both (for i and k-i) and diagonal once. That's fine. Then a_k=(C_k-H_k)/(2a0).

Mirror update:
  def mirror_update(a, lam, eta, floor=0.0):
      d = grad_top(a,lam)
      # d nonnegative but numerical; set eps
      md = np.mean(d)
      # If md small, return a
      ratio = md / np.maximum(d, 1e-300)
      # For numerical stability, log ratio clipped
      log_ratio = np.clip(np.log(ratio), -20, 20)
      loga = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
      new = np.exp(loga + eta*log_ratio)
      new = np.clip(new, 0, None)
      s=new.sum(); if s<1e-300 return a
      return new/s
But if a has very small values, log okay. We can maintain a > tiny. Normalize sum=1. a_i of order maybe 1e-4 to 1e-6; log fine down to 1e-15 maybe. Use floor 1e-300? Can underflow? Use eps=1e-300 not. Better a normalized sum1, min maybe 1e-12; log okay. We can avoid log by ratio**eta; if ratio huge maybe overflow. Use logs.

Define function lse_active_score_grad_x(x, beta):
  a = softmax(x) (using max shift)
  c=conv(a)
  cmax = c.max()
  scaled = beta*(c-cmax)
  exps = np.exp(scaled)
  logsum = np.log(exps.sum())
  L = cmax + logsum/beta
  # We want minimize L (approx max). Score=2n*L (since sum=1). Return 2n*L? For local, scale not matter.
  p=exps/exps.sum()
  d=grad_coeff(a,p)*2? grad of L wrt a = 2*conv_corr(p,a) (same as before with lam=p).
  grad_x = a*(d - np.dot(a,d))
  return value, grad_x

Let's implement flexible lam type:
  def make_lambda(c, gamma=10, top_frac=1.0, mix=0.0):
      Lc=len(c); cmax=max(c); if cmax<=1e-300: return ones/Lc
      if top_frac <1:
         thresh=np.quantile(c, 1-top_frac)
         w=np.zeros(Lc); mask=c>=thresh; z=gamma*(c[mask]/cmax -1); w[mask]=np.exp(z)
      else:
         z=gamma*(c/cmax -1); w=np.exp(z)  # max 1 at cmax, 0 underflow for small.
      sw=w.sum(); if sw<=0: w[ (c==cmax) ]=1; sw=...;
      w=w/sw
      if mix>0:
         w = (1-mix)*w + mix*(1/Lc)
         sw=w.sum(); w/=sw
      return w
  gamma >0. If gamma=0 uniform over masked (or all). top_frac can be 1 for all.

Mirror update code:
  def mirror_update(a, d, eta):
      maxd=d.max(); if maxd<=0 return a
      d_safe=np.maximum(d, maxd*1e-12)
      md=np.mean(d) # arithmetic
      log_ratio=np.log(md/d_safe)
      log_ratio=np.clip(log_ratio, -30, 30)
      loga=np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
      new=np.exp(loga + eta*log_ratio)
      # under/overflow: use shift
      if not finite, fallback ratio**eta
      new=np.clip(new,0,1) # since sum1 max1
      s=new.sum(); if s<=0 return a
      return new/s

1. `clean_normalize(a, eps=1e-12)`: np.asarray, flat, clip min 0, if sum < 1e-12 return ones; else /sum. But for output, we want not necessarily normalized? Score invariant, output normalized OK. To avoid sum <0.01, normalized sum=1.
2. `make_binomial_root(n)`: recurrence. If n=1 returns [1]. Use float64.
3. `conv_square(a)`:
   n=len(a); if n==0 return; L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()? Need length 2n-1. Use `size = 2*n - 1; L=1 << (size-1).bit_length()`. For n=1 size=1 L=1.
   A=np.fft.rfft(a, L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A, L)[:size]; return np.maximum(c,0) maybe small negative due FFT; c=np.real(c). But A*A of real FFT not complex? A is complex; A*A (not abs) squares coefficients, correct (not abs). Use `A * A`. irfft can have tiny negative; max maybe. Do not clip values that are negative? max okay but negative small. For exact max, nonnegative sequence convolution nonnegative; negative FFT noise could become small negative, but max unaffected. Maybe use `np.maximum(c,0)`? That could slightly alter score lower than exact? For internal, OK but final should match. For negative noise, max positive. Clipping c to 0 might improve internal vs actual if small negative? max unaffected unless all negative; no. Use c.real.
4. `score_sequence(a)`:
   n=len(a); S=float(np.sum(a)); if S <0.01? internal inf? For normalized sum1. c=conv_square(a); m=float(np.max(c)); if S<1e-300 return inf; return 2*n*m/(S*S). But to save, if sum normalized return 2n*m? Use general. We can have `score_normalized(a)` with S maybe, but general.
5. `grad_weighted(a, w, factor=2.0)`:
   n=len(a); m=len(w); conv_len=m+n-1; L=1 << (conv_len-1).bit_length(); a_rev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L); W=np.fft.rfft(w, L); g=np.fft.irfft(a_rev*W, L); g=g[n-1: (n-1)+n].real; return factor*g.
   If factor 2 for derivative of square; factor 1 for linear f*u.
6. `make_lambda(c, gamma=10.0, top_frac=0.2, mix=0.3)`:
   cmax = max(c); if cmax <= 1e-300: return np.ones_like(c)/len
   Lc=len(c)
   if top_frac <1.0:
       thresh=np.quantile(c, 1.0-top_frac)
       w=np.zeros(Lc); mask=c>=thresh
       # if no mask, set top indices
       z=gamma*(c[mask]/cmax -1.0)
       w[mask]=np.exp(z)
   else:
       z=gamma*(c/cmax -1.0); w=np.exp(z)
   sw=float(w.sum())
   if not finite or sw<=0:
       w=np.zeros(Lc); idx=int(np.argmax(c)); w[idx]=1; sw=1
   w /= sw
   if mix and mix>0:
       w = (1.0-mix)*w + mix*(1.0/Lc)
       sw=float(w.sum()); w/=sw
   return w
   If gamma=0, z all 0 over mask -> uniform top. If top_frac=1, uniform all with mix uniform. Good.
7. `mirror_update(a, d, eta, target=None, floor_frac=1e-12)`:
   a=np.asarray; if target is None: target=np.dot(a,d)
   if not finite or target<=0: return a.copy()
   maxd=float(np.max(d)); if <=0: return a.copy()
   floor=maxd*floor_frac
   ds=np.maximum(d, floor)
   lr=np.log(target/ds)
   lr=np.clip(lr, -40.0, 40.0)
   la=np.log(np.maximum(a, floor))
   new=np.exp(la + eta*lr)
   # if nonfinite, fallback using np.clip ratio**eta
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(new)):
       ratio=target/ds; ratio=np.clip(ratio, 1e-10, 1e10); new=a*(ratio**eta)
   new=np.clip(new,0.0,1.0)
   s=float(np.sum(new)); if s<=0: return a.copy()
   return new/s

Note: if a has zeros, log floor gives large negative; new may underflow to 0; okay.

def mirror_refine(a0, best_score, rng, t_start, t_end, label=''):
    n=len(a0)

    best_sc=score_sequence(a)

        # If current score not improving within e.g. 200 iterations, change params/noise
        ...

        base_a=a
        base_sc=cur_sc
        chosen=base_a; chosen_sc=base_sc

9. `linearized_update(f, t_end, rng?)`:
   Optimize u wrt f.
```
def linearized_update(f, n_iters=50, gamma=5, top_frac=0.1, mix=0.2, eta=0.2, t_end=None):
    f=clean_normalize(f)
    u=clean_normalize(f.copy())
    # If we want faster, use linear conv each iter
    for it in range(n_iters):
        if t_end and time.monotonic()>t_end: break
        c=conv_linear(f,u) # could use same conv_square? no, f != u. Need function conv_pair(a,b).
```
Need implement conv_pair via FFT length >= len(f)+len(u)-1. Since both length n, same as conv_square length L. We can write `conv_pair(a,b)`: L=next_pow2(2n-1); A=rfft(a,L); B=rfft(b,L); return irfft(A*B,L)[:2n-1]. For square, A*A is pair.
Linear grad wrt u for c=f*u: derivative =∑λ f_{k-j}, which is grad_weighted(f, lam, factor=1) (not u? Our grad_weighted(a,w) computes ∑w_k a_{k-j}; so with a=f,w=λ gives d_j=∑λ f_{k-j}. Correct.)
Update u=mirror_update(u,d,eta) with target=dot(u,d). This minimizes linear q.
Return u.
```
But linear q is homogeneous; scale invariant. We can use same mirror. The linear problem optimum value min max(f*u) with sum u=1. This value V ≤ current max(f*f)=cmax? Since u=f one candidate. It may be smaller. Good.
After u, outer line search:
  for t in [0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.7,1.0?]: new=normalize((1-t)f+t*u); sc=score(new); accept best if < current. After accept, set f=new and repeat outer until time.
We can integrate as an alternative phase.

Which is more likely to improve? The previous SOTA used LP exactly; linearized mirror may approximate. It could improve when direct mirror stuck. We'll include if time.

Function `linearized_refine(f0,best_score,rng,t_start,t_end,label)`:
  f=normalize f0; current score; best.
  u=f
  gamma=5; top_frac=0.1; eta=0.2; stagn=0.
  while time<end:
     # optimize u for maybe 30-100 iterations
     f_cur=f; u=clean_normalize(f_cur)

     else: stagn++; if stagn>3: gamma*=1.2; top_frac*=0.7; eta*=0.7; reset u=f.
  return best.

10. L-BFGS LSE with softmax x:
```
def lse_x_value_grad(x, beta):
    a=softmax(x)
    c=conv_square(a)
    cmax=max(c)
    if cmax <=0: return 0, zeros
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    # z max 0; exp ok
    ez=np.exp(z)
    sumez=float(ez.sum())
    L=cmax + np.log(sumez)/beta
    p=ez/sumez
    d=grad_weighted(a,p,2.0)
    adotd=float(np.dot(a,d))
    gx=a*(d-adotd)
    return (2*n*L), gx
```
   But this functions get beta arg. Need top-level with signature `lse_objective_x(x, beta)`. We also need n? n from x.
Potential numerical: beta*(c-cmax) may underflow; p only maxes. Good. If all c zero? no.
Scipy minimize can call with x array and beta. Use args=(beta,).
```
def run_lbfgs_x(a0, beta, maxiter, maxfun, ftol, time_limit optional)
    a=clean_normalize(a0); x=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); x-=x.mean()
    res=minimize(lse_objective_x, x, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':...,'ftol':1e-14,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':...,'maxls':20})
    a_out=softmax(res.x); sc=score_sequence(a_out); return sc,a_out
```
But L-BFGS-B for unbounded x; options maxfun. We need time limit; minimize no time callback. We can estimate by maxiter. Use small maxiter e.g. 500 for 7000 dims? Each iteration may ~0.01-0.05, 500 ~25s. Good. Try at most 3 betas.

Since objective value ~1.5, ftol maybe 1e-13. Use `disp=False`.

def gerchberg_refine(a0,best_score,rng,t_start,t_end,label):
  a=norm; best...
  N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
  uniform = np.zeros(N); lev=1/(2n-1); uniform[:2n-1]=lev
  beta=0.2
  relax=0.5
  it=0
  while time<end:
     F=rfft(a,N)
     F2=F*F
     T_target=(1-beta)*? Wait c current time = ifft(F2)[:L]. Instead compute c=conv_square(a) (already maybe). T=(1-beta)*c_ext + beta*uniform
     Tf=rfft(T,N)
     # ratio = sqrt(Tf/F2)
     denom=F2; ratio=np.sqrt(Tf/denom)
     Fnew=F*ratio
     # Or Fnew = F * (Tf/F2)^0.5, if F2 zero: set Fnew contribution zero? Use safe.
     new=irfft(Fnew,N)[:n].real
     new=np.maximum(new,0)
     # line search mix: for alpha in [relax,0.5,...]
     for tau in [relax,0.2,0.1,0.5,1.0]:
         cand=clean_normalize((1-tau)*a + tau*new)
         sc=score(cand)
         accept best
     # adjust beta: if improve beta *=1.1; stagn beta*=0.7 or perturb
  return best
```

Linearized LP via MWT details:
Problem: given f, solve min_{u,sum=1,u≥0} max_k (f*u)_k. This is a linear minimax. Multiplicative weights on u with payoff? The loss for u if adversary chooses k is (f*u)_k. We want minimize max. Pseudo:
```
def linearized_refine(f0, best_score, rng, t_start, t_end):
    n=len(f0); f=norm(f0)
    Lc=next_pow2(2*n-1); Lg=next_pow2(3*n-2)
    Ff_c = rfft(f,Lc)
    Ffr_g = rfft(f[::-1],Lg)
    u=f.copy()
    for iters? within while:
       # MWT for u maybe 50-200 steps based on time
       gamma=5; top_frac=0.1; mix=0.2; eta=0.1
       for inner in range(n_inner):
          U=rfft(u,Lc); c=irfft(Ff_c*U,Lc)[:2n-1]
          lam=make_lambda(c,gamma,top_frac,mix)
          Wr=rfft(lam,Lg); g=irfft(Ffr_g*Wr,Lg)[n-1:2n-1]; d=g  # no factor 2
          u=mirror_update(u,d,eta)
       # line search mix
       for t in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.8]: cand=norm((1-t)f+t*u); sc=score...
       if improved: f=best; adjust gamma/top
       else: perturb u/f
    return best
```

```
def resample_sequence(a, n):
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n); return np.maximum(np.interp(x_new,x_old,a),0)
```

Define softmax:
```
def softmax_prob(x):
    z=x - x.max()
    e=np.exp(z)
    s=e.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return np.ones(len(x))/n
    return e/s
```

  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  for s in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0]: w=1+0.3*np.exp(-x/s); base*w
  for p in [0.5,1,2]: w=1+0.3*x**p; ...

```

def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    t0=time.monotonic(); total=float(budget_s); deadline=t0+total-60.0

    parent=None

           p=height_sequence_1

    best_a=None; best_sc=np.inf

    else:

    # unique sorted

    for n in test_lengths:
       a=make_binomial_root(n); sc=score_sequence(a); update if better
       # windows if time
       if time>deadline-?? continue
       x=np.linspace(0,1,n)

           a=make_binomial_root(n)*(1+0.25*np.exp(-x/s)); a=clean_normalize(a); sc=score; update
       for t in [0.2,0.5]:
           a=make_binomial_root(n)*(1 + t*x - t*np.exp(-x/0.02)); if norm; update
    # If parent and length n0, include parent
    if parent is not None:
       a=clean_normalize(parent); sc=score; update
    # Maybe parent window if n0
    ...
    # Main mirror
    if time < deadline-400:
       t_end=min(deadline-400, t0+0.5*(total-60))
       a_best,sc=mirror_refine(best_a,best_sc,rng,t0,t_end,'mirror')

          m=n+dn; if m<1024 or m>20000: continue

`mirror_update`: `np.exp(la + eta*lr)` might underflow/overflow; we shift? We can subtract max exponent to prevent overflow:

lr = np.log(target/(d+floor))
exps = la + eta*lr; exps -= exps.max(); new=np.exp(exps); new/=sum
```

Detailed code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()

def clean_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return np.ones(1)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a,0.0,None)
    s=a.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones(a.size)/a.size
    return a/s
```
But for output, we might want sum not normalized? It's okay. For clean with no normalize? We'll use.

`make_binomial_root(n)`:
```
a=np.zeros(n); if n==0 return a; a[0]=1.0
for i in range(n-1): a[i+1]=a[i]*(2*i+1)/(2*i+2)
# optional scale? not needed
return a
```
For n large, a_i can become ~0.1? yes.

`conv_square(a)`:
```
n=a.size; L=next_pow2(2*n-1); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*A,L); c=c[:2*n-1]; c=np.real(c); return c
```
Maybe A*A can have tiny imaginary from round; product complex. irfft returns real.

`conv_pair(a,b)`:
 length maybe same n. General:
```
n=len(a); m=len(b); L=next_pow2(n+m-1); A=np.fft.rfft(a,L); B=np.fft.rfft(b,L); c=np.fft.irfft(A*B,L)[:n+m-1]; return real
```
In linearized both n.

`score_sequence(a)`:
```
a=np.asarray...; s=float(np.sum(a)); if s<1e-12: return np.inf
n=a.size; c=conv_square(a); m=float(np.max(c)); if m<0: m=0? return 2*n*m/(s*s)
```
Could be slow for many candidates; okay.

For mirror loops, we need score with current sum. Could define `score_from_c(a,c)` to avoid conv.
```
def score_from_c(a,c): s=a.sum(); ...
```

`grad_weighted(a,w,factor=2.0)`:
 Need if len(w) maybe 2n-1. If w length differs, conv_len = len(w)+len(a)-1. slice start = n-1? This assumes w is convolution coefficient indices 0..2n-1 and a length n. If w length not 2n-1, formula? We only use same. Keep.
```
n=a.size; L=next_pow2(len(w)+n-1); Arev=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],L); W=np.fft.rfft(w,L); g=np.fft.irfft(Arev*W,L); g=g[n-1:n-1+n]; return factor*np.real(g)
```
If len(w) not 2n-1, slice may out of bounds? Ensure length.

`make_lambda(c,gamma=5.0,top_frac=0.2,mix=0.2)`:
```
c=np.asarray(c,float); Lc=c.size; cmax=float(np.max(c))
if cmax <= 1e-300: return np.ones(Lc)/Lc
if top_frac is None or top_frac >=1:
   z=gamma*(c/cmax -1.0); w=np.exp(z)
else:
   thresh=float(np.quantile(c, 1.0-top_frac))
   mask=c>=thresh
   if not mask.any(): mask[c.argmax()]=True
   z=np.zeros(Lc); z[mask]=gamma*(c[mask]/cmax -1.0)
   w=np.exp(z)
sw=float(w.sum())
if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <=0:
   w=np.zeros(Lc); w[int(np.argmax(c))]=1.0; sw=1.0
w=w/sw
if mix>0:
   w=(1.0-mix)*w + mix*(1.0/Lc)
   sw=float(w.sum()); if sw>0: w/=sw
return w
```
If gamma negative? We'll use nonnegative. If gamma=0 and top mask, uniform over top.

`mirror_update(a,d,eta)`:
```
def mirror_update(a,d,eta,target=None):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); d=np.asarray(d,float)
   if eta <=0 or d.size!=a.size or a.size==0: return a.copy()
   if target is None: target=float(np.dot(a,d))
   maxd=float(np.max(d))
   if not np.isfinite(target) or target <=0 or not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd <=0: return a.copy()
   floor=max(1e-300, maxd*1e-12)
   ds=d.copy(); ds[ds<floor]=floor
   ratio=target/ds
   # avoid overflow
   ratio=np.clip(ratio, 1e-20, 1e20)
   loga=np.log(a+floor)
   lr=np.log(ratio)
   exps=loga + eta*lr
   mx=float(np.max(exps))
   new=np.exp(exps - mx)
   s=float(np.sum(new))
   if s <=0 or not np.isfinite(s): return a.copy()
   new=new/s
   return np.clip(new,0,1)
```
If a has sum 1, max<=1; new normalized.

But if `target` is weighted mean, ratio can be huge for d low. OK.

`mirror_refine` final code. Need no nested functions; use top-level helpers.
Let's write:
```
def mirror_refine(a0, best_score, rng, t_start, t_end, label=''):
    n=a0.size
    a=clean_normalize(a0.copy())
    best_a=a.copy(); best_sc=score_sequence(a)
    gamma=4.0; top_frac=0.25; mix=0.3; eta=0.18
    stagn=0; it=0; last_progress=0
    print(f"mirror {label} start n={n} best={best_sc:.8f}")
    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        c=conv_square(a)
        s=float(a.sum())
        cur_sc=2.0*n*max(0.0,float(np.max(c)))/(s*s)
        if cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc=cur_sc; best_a=a.copy(); last_progress=it
        # if stagnation
        if it - last_progress > 100:
            # adjust
            gamma=min(gamma*1.4,60.0)
            top_frac=max(top_frac*0.7,0.01)
            mix=max(mix*0.6,0.0)
            eta=max(eta*0.7,0.02)
            a=clean_normalize(a*(1.0+0.05*rng.standard_normal(n)))
            # small blend to base maybe
            if rng.random()<0.25:
                base=make_binomial_root(n)
                a=clean_normalize(0.75*a+0.25*base)
            it=last_progress #? keep count
        lam=make_lambda(c,gamma,top_frac,mix)
        d=grad_weighted(a,lam,2.0)
        base_sc=cur_sc
        chosen=a; chosen_sc=cur_sc
        et=eta
        for _ in range(5):
            cand=mirror_update(a,d,et)
            sc=score_sequence(cand)
            if sc < chosen_sc:
                chosen=cand; chosen_sc=sc
            et *= 0.5
        if chosen_sc < base_sc:
            a=chosen
            # if accepted after small et, maybe keep eta; if after first, increase slightly
            eta=min(eta*1.05, 1.0)
            stagn=0
        else:
            stagn += 1
            if stagn > 10:
                gamma = min(max(gamma*1.5, 1.0), 100.0)
                top_frac=max(top_frac*0.75,0.005)
                mix=max(mix*0.5,0.0)
                eta=0.05
                a=clean_normalize(a*(1.0+0.1*rng.standard_normal(n)))
                if rng.random()<0.3:
                    base=make_binomial_root(n)
                    a=clean_normalize(0.6*a+0.4*base)
                stagn=0
        it +=1
        if it % 250 ==0:
            gamma=min(gamma*1.15,100.0)
            top_frac=max(top_frac*0.9,0.005)
            mix=max(mix*0.9,0.0)
            print(f"  {label} iter={it} best={best_sc:.8f} curr={2.0*n*np.max(c)/(a.sum()**2):.8f} eta={eta:.3f} gamma={gamma:.1f} top={top_frac:.4f}")
    final_sc=score_sequence(best_a)
    if final_sc < best_sc: best_sc=final_sc
    return best_a,best_sc
```
Potential: after changing a due stagnation, base_sc computed from old c? We continue; fine.

def linearized_refine(a0,best_score,rng,t_start,t_end,label=''):
 n=len; f=clean_normalize(a0); best=...
 Lc=next_pow2(2*n-1); Lg=next_pow2(3*n-2)
 gamma=6; top_frac=0.1; mix=0.2; eta=0.15; inner_iters=80; stag=0
 while time<end:
   Ff_c=np.fft.rfft(f,Lc)
   Ffr_g=np.fft.rfft(f[::-1],Lg)
   u=clean_normalize(f)
   # Warm start u maybe previous? Keep global u outside loop. Use u from previous if finite.
   for inner in range(inner_iters):
      if time>end: break
      U=np.fft.rfft(u,Lc)
      c=np.fft.irfft(Ff_c*U,Lc)[:2*n-1].real
      lam=make_lambda(c,gamma,top_frac,mix)
      Wg=np.fft.rfft(lam,Lg)
      g=np.fft.irfft(Ffr_g*Wg,Lg)[n-1:2*n-1].real
      u=mirror_update(u,g,eta)
   # line search
   base_sc=score_sequence(f)
   chosen=f; chosen_sc=base_sc
   for t in (0.05,0.1,0.2,0.3,0.5,0.75,1.0):
       cand=clean_normalize((1-t)*f + t*u)
       sc=score_sequence(cand)
       if sc<chosen_sc:
          chosen=cand; chosen_sc=sc
   if chosen_sc < best_sc: best...
   if chosen_sc < base_sc:
       f=chosen; stag=0; gamma*=1.1; top_frac=max(top_frac*0.9,0.01)
   else:
       stag +=1
       if stag>2:
          gamma*=1.3; top_frac*=0.7; eta*=0.7; f=clean_normalize(f*(1+0.05*rng.normal(n))); stag=0
   print progress
 return best
```
Need keep u warm across outer? Could set `u_global=f.copy()` before while, and after line search maybe `u_global=u`. Use global variable local.

 else: u_global=u.copy()
```

`gerchberg_refine`: implement as extra.

def gerchberg_refine(a0,best_score,rng,t_start,t_end,label=''):
 n; a=norm; best...
 L=next_pow2(2*n-1); Lc=2*n-1
 uniform=np.zeros(L); lev=1.0/Lc; uniform[:Lc]=lev
 beta=0.15; tau=0.25; stag=0; it=0
 while time<end:
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    F2=F*F
    c=np.fft.irfft(F2,L)[:Lc].real
    target=(1-beta)*c + beta*uniform
    # ensure target nonneg
    target=np.maximum(target,0)
    # sum target maybe same if c sum? conv_square sum = (a.sum())^2=1; uniform sum=1; good.
    Tf=np.fft.rfft(target,L)
    denom=F2
    phi=np.zeros_like(denom, dtype=complex)
    mask=np.abs(denom)>1e-200
    phi[mask]=np.sqrt(Tf[mask]/denom[mask])
    phi[~mask]=np.sqrt(Tf[~mask]) # maybe
    Fnew=F*phi + 0? Actually if mask false, F is zero, F*0=0, plus sqrt.
    # But if F=0 and Tf=0, sqrt 0.
    # Relax in frequency: Frelax=(1-tau)*F + tau*Fnew? Maybe time domain better.
    new=np.fft.irfft(Fnew,L)[:n].real
    new=np.maximum(new,0)
    # line search tau
    chosen=a; chosen_sc=score_sequence(a)
    for rel in (tau, 0.1, 0.3, 0.5, 1.0):
       cand=clean_normalize((1-rel)*a + rel*new)
       sc=score_sequence(cand)
       if sc<chosen_sc: chosen=cand; chosen_sc=sc
    if chosen_sc<best_sc: update; reset stag
    if chosen_sc < old_sc:
       a=chosen; beta=min(beta*1.1,0.6); tau=max(tau*0.8,0.05)
    else:
       stag++; if stag>5: beta*=0.8; tau*=0.7; perturb
    it++; anneal every 200
 return best
```

Implement:
```
def lse_x_value_grad(x, beta):
    a=softmax_prob(x)
    c=conv_square(a)
    cmax=float(np.max(c))
    n=len(x)
    if cmax <=0: return 0.0, np.zeros_like(x)
    z=beta*(c-cmax)
    exp=np.exp(z)
    sume=float(exp.sum())
    if sume <=0 or not finite:
       p=np.zeros(c.size); p[int(np.argmax(c))]=1
    else:
       p=exp/sume
    L=cmax+np.log(sume if sume>0 else 1)/beta
    d=grad_weighted(a,p,2.0)
    ad=float(np.dot(a,d))
    gx=a*(d-ad)
    val=2*n*L
    if not np.isfinite(val): val=1e10
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)): gx=np.zeros_like(x)
    return val,gx
```
If sume zero underflow: p one-hot. `np.log(sume)` huge negative; but z max 0, at least one exp 1, so sume>=1. Good.
`mse`: beta large, c-cmax negative; exp of -1000 underflow but max 1. OK.

res=minimize(lse_x_value_grad,x,args=(beta,),method='L-BFGS-B',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-12,'maxfun':maxfun,'maxls':20,'disp':0})
a_out=softmax_prob(res.x)
sc=score_sequence(a_out); if sc<inf return else a0.
```

```python
"""
Search for non-negative steps minimizing
    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Main ingredients:
- cheap exact convolutions and subgradients by FFT;
- a multiplicative mirror-descent method on the simplex, with adaptive
  weights on the tight convolution peaks;
- a linearized subproblem (given f, minimize max(f*u)) solved by the same
  mirror method, then mixed back into f;
- a Gerchberg-Saxton-like frequency-domain square-root update;
- short L-BFGS refinements of the log-sum-exp smoothed objective on a
  softmax-normalized simplex.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def _next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _clean_normalize(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _make_binomial_root(n):
    a = np.zeros(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for i in range(int(n) - 1):
        a[i + 1] = a[i] * (2.0 * i + 1.0) / (2.0 * i + 2.0)
    return a


def _conv_square(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = 2 * n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * A, L)
    c = c[:size].real
    return c


def _conv_pair(a, b):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = b.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    size = n + m - 1
    L = _next_pow2(size)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(A * B, L)
    return c[:size].real


def _score_sequence(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-14:
        return np.inf
    c = _conv_square(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m / (s * s))


def _grad_weighted(a, w, factor=2.0):
    """
    Grad = factor * g where
        g[j] = sum_k w[k] a[k-j], 0 <= k-j < len(a).
    For factor=2 this is the gradient of conv(a,a) under weights w.
    For factor=1 this is the gradient of conv(f,u) with respect to u.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = a.size
    m = w.size
    if n == 0 or m == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    conv_len = m + n - 1
    L = _next_pow2(conv_len)
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], L)
    W = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    g = np.fft.irfft(Ar * W, L)
    g = g[n - 1:n - 1 + n].real
    return float(factor) * g


def _make_lambda(c, gamma=5.0, top_frac=0.2, mix=0.2):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    Lc = c.size
    if Lc == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return np.ones(Lc, dtype=np.float64) / float(Lc)

    gamma = float(gamma)
    top_frac = float(top_frac)
    mix = float(mix)
    if gamma < 0.0:
        gamma = 0.0
    if top_frac <= 0.0:
        top_frac = 1.0
    if top_frac > 1.0:
        top_frac = 1.0

    if top_frac >= 1.0:
        z = gamma * (c / cmax - 1.0)
        w = np.exp(z)
    else:
        thresh = float(np.quantile(c, 1.0 - top_frac))
        mask = c >= thresh
        if not mask.any():
            mask[np.argmax(c)] = True
        z = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
        z[mask] = gamma * (c[mask] / cmax - 1.0)
        w = np.exp(z)

    sw = float(w.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(sw) or sw <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(Lc, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        sw = 1.0
    w = w / sw

    if mix > 0.0:
        w = (1.0 - mix) * w + mix * (1.0 / float(Lc))
        sw = float(w.sum())
        if sw > 0.0:
            w = w / sw
    return w


def _mirror_update(a, d, eta):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0 or d.size != a.size or eta <= 0.0:
        return a.copy()
    if not (np.all(np.isfinite(d)) and np.all(np.isfinite(a))):
        return a.copy()

    target = float(np.dot(a, d))
    maxd = float(np.max(d))
    if target <= 0.0 or maxd <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(target) or not np.isfinite(maxd):
        return a.copy()

    floor = max(1e-300, maxd * 1e-12)
    ds = np.maximum(d, floor)
    ratio = target / ds
    ratio = np.clip(ratio, 1e-20, 1e20)

    la = np.log(a + floor)
    lr = np.log(ratio)
    e = la + float(eta) * lr
    e -= float(np.max(e))
    new = np.exp(e)
    s = float(np.sum(new))
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return a.copy()
    new = new / s
    return np.clip(new, 0.0, None)


def _softmax_prob(x):
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    x = x - float(np.max(x))
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(x.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(x.size)
    return e / s


def _lse_x_value_grad(x, beta):
    a = _softmax_prob(x)
    n = a.size
    c = _conv_square(a)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if cmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(cmax):
        return 0.0, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    beta = float(beta)
    if beta <= 0.0:
        beta = 1.0

    z = beta * (c - cmax)
    ez = np.exp(z)
    se = float(ez.sum())
    if se <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(se):
        p = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
        p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
    else:
        p = ez / se

    L = cmax + float(np.log(max(se, 1e-300))) / beta
    d = _grad_weighted(a, p, 2.0)
    ad = float(np.dot(a, d))
    gx = a * (d - ad)

    if not np.all(np.isfinite(gx)):
        gx = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    val = 2.0 * n * L
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e12
    return float(val), gx


def _update_best(a, sc, best_a, best_sc, label=""):
    if a is None:
        return best_a, best_sc
    sc = float(sc)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return best_a, best_sc
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        return best_a, best_sc
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if sc < best_sc:
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_sc = sc
        print(f"  NEW BEST {label}: {sc:.10f} (n={a.size}, t={time.monotonic():.1f})")
    return best_a, best_sc


def _resample_sequence(a, n):
    a = _clean_normalize(a)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x1, x0, a)
    return np.maximum(out, 0.0)


def _mirror_refine(a0, best_score, rng, t_start, t_end, label="mirror"):
    if a0 is None or a0.size == 0:
        return _clean_normalize(_make_binomial_root(64)), best_score
    n = int(a0.size)
    a = _clean_normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_sequence(a)
    if best_sc >= best_score:
        best_a = np.asarray(a0, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        best_sc = best_score

    modes = [
        (2.0, 0.50, 0.50, 0.30),
        (5.0, 0.25, 0.30, 0.20),
        (10.0, 0.12, 0.15, 0.12),
        (25.0, 0.05, 0.05, 0.08),
        (60.0, 0.02, 0.00, 0.05),
        (90.0, 0.01, 0.00, 0.03),
    ]
    mode_idx = 0
    gamma, top_frac, mix, eta = modes[mode_idx]
    it = 0
    last_progress = 0
    stagn = 0

    print(f"  {label}: start n={n}, best={best_sc:.10f}")

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        c = _conv_square(a)
        s = float(a.sum())
        cmax = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        cur_sc = 2.0 * n * max(0.0, cmax) / (s * s)
        if not np.isfinite(cur_sc):
            cur_sc = 1e12

        if cur_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            last_progress = it

        if it - last_progress > 90 or stagn > 25:
            mode_idx = (mode_idx + 1) % len(modes)
            gamma, top_frac, mix, eta = modes[mode_idx]
            if stagn > 0:
                a = _clean_normalize(a * (1.0 + 0.04 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
                if rng.random() < 0.25:
                    base = _make_binomial_root(n)
                    a = _clean_normalize(0.7 * a + 0.3 * base)
            stagn = 0

        lam = _make_lambda(c, gamma=gamma, top_frac=top_frac, mix=mix)
        d = _grad_weighted(a, lam, 2.0)

        base_sc = cur_sc
        chosen = a
        chosen_sc = cur_sc
        et = eta
        for _ in range(4):
            cand = _mirror_update(a, d, et)
            sc = _score_sequence(cand)
            if sc < chosen_sc:
                chosen_sc = sc
                chosen = cand
            et *= 0.5

        if chosen_sc < base_sc:
            a = chosen
            eta = min(eta * 1.06, 1.2)
            it += 1
        else:
            stagn += 1
            if stagn > 10:
                gamma = min(max(gamma * 1.3, 0.5), 120.0)
                top_frac = max(float(top_frac) * 0.75, 0.005)
                mix = max(float(mix) * 0.55, 0.0)
                eta = max(float(eta) * 0.7, 0.02)
                a = _clean_normalize(a * (1.0 + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
                if rng.random() < 0.35:
                    base = _make_binomial_root(n)
                    a = _clean_normalize(0.6 * a + 0.4 * base)
                stagn = 0
            it += 1

        if it % 250 == 0:
            print(
                f"    {label} iter={it} best={best_sc:.10f} curr={cur_sc:.10f} "
                f"eta={eta:.3f} gamma={gamma:.1f} top={top_frac:.4f}"
            )

    final_sc = _score_sequence(best_a)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = final_sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def _linearized_refine(a0, best_score, rng, t_start, t_end, label="linear"):
    if a0 is None or a0.size == 0:
        return _clean_normalize(_make_binomial_root(64)), best_score
    n = int(a0.size)
    f = _clean_normalize(a0)
    best_a = _clean_normalize(a0)
    best_sc = _score_sequence(best_a)

    modes = [
        (6.0, 0.20, 0.30, 0.20, 35),
        (12.0, 0.10, 0.20, 0.15, 55),
        (30.0, 0.04, 0.05, 0.10, 80),
        (80.0, 0.01, 0.00, 0.06, 100),
    ]
    mode_idx = 0
    gamma, top_frac, mix, eta, inner_n = modes[mode_idx]
    u = f.copy()
    stag_outer = 0

    print(f"  {label}: start n={n}, best={best_sc:.10f}")

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        Lc = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
        Lg = _next_pow2(3 * n - 2)
        Ff_c = np.fft.rfft(f, Lc)
        Ffr_g = np.fft.rfft(f[::-1], Lg)

        u = _clean_normalize(u)
        for _ in range(int(inner_n)):
            if time.monotonic() > t_end:
                break
            U = np.fft.rfft(u, Lc)
            c = np.fft.irfft(Ff_c * U, Lc)[:2 * n - 1].real
            lam = _make_lambda(c, gamma=gamma, top_frac=top_frac, mix=mix)
            Wg = np.fft.rfft(lam, Lg)
            g = np.fft.irfft(Ffr_g * Wg, Lg)[n - 1:2 * n - 1].real
            u = _mirror_update(u, g, eta)

        base_sc = _score_sequence(f)
        chosen = f
        chosen_sc = base_sc
        for t in (0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.8):
            cand = _clean_normalize((1.0 - t) * f + t * u)
            sc = _score_sequence(cand)
            if sc < chosen_sc:
                chosen_sc = sc
                chosen = cand

        if chosen_sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = chosen_sc
            best_a = chosen.copy()

        if chosen_sc < base_sc:
            f = chosen
            u = _clean_normalize(u)
            stag_outer = 0
            mode_idx = (mode_idx + 1) % len(modes)
        else:
            stag_outer += 1
            if stag_outer > 2:
                mode_idx = (mode_idx + 1) % len(modes)
                f = _clean_normalize(f * (1.0 + 0.05 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
                u = f.copy()
                stag_outer = 0

        gamma, top_frac, mix, eta, inner_n = modes[mode_idx]
        if time.monotonic() >= t_end:
            break

    final_sc = _score_sequence(best_a)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = final_sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def _gerchberg_refine(a0, best_score, rng, t_start, t_end, label="gerchberg"):
    if a0 is None or a0.size == 0:
        return _clean_normalize(_make_binomial_root(64)), best_score
    n = int(a0.size)
    a = _clean_normalize(a0)
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_sequence(a)

    L = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    Lc = 2 * n - 1
    uniform = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    uniform[:Lc] = 1.0 / float(Lc)

    beta = 0.18
    cap_frac = 0.82
    relax = 0.25
    stag = 0
    it = 0

    print(f"  {label}: start n={n}, best={best_sc:.10f}")

    while time.monotonic() < t_end:
        try:
            F = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
            F2 = F * F
            c = np.fft.irfft(F2, L)[:Lc].real
            c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
            csum = float(c.sum())
            if csum > 0.0:
                c = c / csum
            else:
                c = uniform[:Lc].copy()

            cmax = float(np.max(c))
            if it % 2 == 0:
                target = (1.0 - beta) * c + beta * uniform[:Lc]
            else:
                target = c.copy()
                cap = max(cmax * cap_frac, 1e-12)
                target[target > cap] = cap
                deficit = 1.0 - float(target.sum())
                if deficit > 0.0:
                    target = target + (deficit / float(Lc)) * uniform[:Lc]
                    target = np.maximum(target, 0.0)
                    tsum = float(target.sum())
                    if tsum > 0.0:
                        target = target / tsum

            Tf = np.fft.rfft(target, L)
            Fnew = np.zeros_like(F, dtype=np.complex128)
            mask = np.abs(F2) > 1e-200
            if mask.any():
                Fnew[mask] = F[mask] * np.sqrt(Tf[mask] / F2[mask])
            if (not mask).any():
                Fnew[not mask] = np.sqrt(Tf[not mask])

            new = np.fft.irfft(Fnew, L)[:n].real
            new = np.maximum(new, 0.0)
            snew = float(new.sum())
            if snew <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(snew):
                stag += 1
                it += 1
                continue
            new = new / snew

            base_sc = _score_sequence(a)
            chosen = a
            chosen_sc = base_sc
            for rel in (relax, 0.1, 0.25, 0.5, 1.0):
                cand = _clean_normalize((1.0 - rel) * a + rel * new)
                sc = _score_sequence(cand)
                if sc < chosen_sc:
                    chosen_sc = sc
                    chosen = cand

            if chosen_sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = chosen_sc
                best_a = chosen.copy()

            if chosen_sc < base_sc:
                a = chosen
                beta = min(beta * 1.08, 0.75)
                cap_frac = min(cap_frac * 0.99, 0.45)
                relax = max(relax * 0.92, 0.05)
                stag = 0
                it += 1
            else:
                stag += 1
                if stag > 6:
                    beta = max(beta * 0.75, 0.03)
                    cap_frac = max(cap_frac * 0.9, 0.20)
                    relax = max(relax * 0.7, 0.03)
                    a = _clean_normalize(a * (1.0 + 0.07 * rng.standard_normal(n)))
                    stag = 0
                    it += 1
        except Exception:
            break

    final_sc = _score_sequence(best_a)
    if final_sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = final_sc
    return best_a, best_sc


def _lbfgs_refine_x(a0, beta, maxiter=400, maxfun=5000, label="lbfgs"):
    if a0 is None or a0.size == 0:
        return _clean_normalize(_make_binomial_root(64)), np.inf
    a = _clean_normalize(a0)
    sc0 = _score_sequence(a)
    x = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    x -= float(np.mean(x))

    res = minimize(
        _lse_x_value_grad,
        x,
        args=(beta,),
        method="L-BFGS-B",
        jac=True,
        options={
            "maxiter": int(maxiter),
            "ftol": 1e-15,
            "gtol": 1e-12,
            "maxfun": int(maxfun),
            "maxls": 20,
            "disp": False,
        },
    )
    out = _softmax_prob(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64))
    sc = _score_sequence(out)
    if not np.isfinite(sc) or sc >= sc0:
        return a, sc0
    print(f"  {label} beta={beta}: {sc:.10f}")
    return out, sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400.0):
    """
    Returns a list of non-negative heights for the step function.
    """
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    total = float(budget_s)
    useful = total - 60.0
    if useful <= 30.0:
        useful = max(30.0, total * 0.9)
    deadline = t_start + useful

    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = None
    try:
        p = globals().get("height_sequence_1")
        if p is not None:
            parent = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            parent = np.where(np.isfinite(parent), parent, 0.0)
            parent = np.clip(parent, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if parent.size > 14000:
                parent = _resample_sequence(parent, 14000)
            if parent.size < 64:
                parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None

    if parent is not None:
        n0 = int(parent.size)
    else:
        n0 = 6962

    best_a = None
    best_sc = np.inf

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Initial constructions and quick raw evaluations.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    if parent is not None:
        a = _clean_normalize(parent)
        sc = _score_sequence(a)
        best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a, sc, best_a, best_sc, f"parent n={a.size}")

    lengths = [n0]
    for mult in (0.50, 0.75, 1.00, 1.25, 1.50, 2.00):
        lengths.append(int(round(n0 * mult)))
    for extra in (4096, 8192, 10240):
        lengths.append(extra)

    seen = set()
    clean_lengths = []
    for m in lengths:
        m = int(m)
        if 2048 <= m <= 14000 and m not in seen:
            seen.add(m)
            clean_lengths.append(m)
    clean_lengths = sorted(clean_lengths)[:6]

    print(f"Initial lengths: {clean_lengths}")

    for n in clean_lengths:
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 180.0:
            break
        base = _make_binomial_root(n)
        a = _clean_normalize(base)
        best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a, _score_sequence(a), best_a, best_sc, f"base n={n}")

        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

        for s in (0.08, 0.25, 0.80):
            w = 1.0 + 0.35 * np.exp(-x / s)
            a = _clean_normalize(base * w)
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a, _score_sequence(a), best_a, best_sc, f"window-e^{-x/s} s={s} n={n}")

        for p in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0):
            w = 1.0 + 0.35 * (x ** p)
            a = _clean_normalize(base * w)
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a, _score_sequence(a), best_a, best_sc, f"ramp x^p p={p} n={n}")

        for z in (0.25, 0.60):
            w = np.maximum(1.0 - z * np.exp(-x / 0.02) + z * x, 0.05)
            a = _clean_normalize(base * w)
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a, _score_sequence(a), best_a, best_sc, f"edge z={z} n={n}")

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = _clean_normalize(_make_binomial_root(n0))
        best_sc = _score_sequence(best_a)

    print(f"After initialization: best={best_sc:.10f}, n={best_a.size}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Main multiplicative mirror search.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    mirror_end = t_start + 0.65 * useful
    if time.monotonic() < mirror_end:
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _mirror_refine(
                best_a, best_sc, rng, t_start, mirror_end, label=f"mirror n={best_a.size}"
            )
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, best_sc, "after-mirror")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"mirror failed: {exc}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Linearized subproblem iteration.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    linear_end = t_start + 0.80 * useful
    if time.monotonic() < linear_end:
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _linearized_refine(
                best_a, best_sc, rng, t_start, linear_end, label=f"linear n={best_a.size}"
            )
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, best_sc, "after-linear")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"linear failed: {exc}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Frequency square-root / Gerchberg type update.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    gs_end = t_start + 0.87 * useful
    if time.monotonic() < gs_end:
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _gerchberg_refine(
                best_a, best_sc, rng, t_start, gs_end, label=f"gerchberg n={best_a.size}"
            )
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, best_sc, "after-gerchberg")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"gerchberg failed: {exc}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # L-BFGS refinements on the log-sum-exp-smoothed max coefficient.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def _lbfgs_run(a_in, beta, maxiter, maxfun, tag):
        nonlocal best_a, best_sc
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 40.0:
            return
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_refine_x(a_in, beta, maxiter=maxiter, maxfun=maxfun, label=tag)
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, sc, "after-lbfgs")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"lbfgs {tag} failed: {exc}")

    # The helper above relies on a closure? It was defined inside propose_candidate.
    # To honor the "no closures" rule, replace by direct calls below.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 90.0:
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_refine_x(best_a, 1000.0, maxiter=350, maxfun=4500, label="lbfgs-beta1k")
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, best_sc, "after-lbfgs-1k")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"lbfgs 1k failed: {exc}")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 60.0:
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_refine_x(best_a, 5000.0, maxiter=250, maxfun=3500, label="lbfgs-beta5k")
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, best_sc, "after-lbfgs-5k")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"lbfgs 5k failed: {exc}")

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 35.0:
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _lbfgs_refine_x(best_a, 20000.0, maxiter=150, maxfun=2000, label="lbfgs-beta20k")
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, best_sc, "after-lbfgs-20k")
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"lbfgs 20k failed: {exc}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final tiny length rescans; sometimes integer length changes help.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    n_best = int(best_a.size)
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 20.0:
        for dn in (-1024, -512, -256, 256, 512, 1024, 2048):
            if time.monotonic() > deadline - 15.0:
                break
            m = n_best + int(dn)
            if m < 2048 or m > 20000:
                continue
            a2 = _clean_normalize(_resample_sequence(best_a, m))
            sc2 = _score_sequence(a2)
            best_a, best_sc = _update_best(a2, sc2, best_a, best_sc, f"resample dn={dn}")

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Final safety verification.
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    out_arr = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if out_arr.size == 0:
        out_arr = _clean_normalize(_make_binomial_root(64))
    out_arr = np.where(np.isfinite(out_arr), out_arr, 0.0)
    out_arr = np.clip(out_arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if float(out_arr.sum()) < 0.01:
        out_arr = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)

    out = [float(v) for v in out_arr]
    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
    except Exception:
        verified = np.inf

    if not np.isfinite(verified):
        if parent is not None:
            out = [float(max(0.0, min(1000.0, v))) for v in np.asarray(parent, dtype=np.float64)]
            try:
                verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            except Exception:
                verified = np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(verified):
            out = [float(v) for v in _clean_normalize(_make_binomial_root(64))]
            try:
                verified = float(evaluate_sequence(out))
            except Exception:
                verified = np.inf

    elapsed = time.monotonic() - t_start
    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal={best_sc:.12f}, verified={verified:.12f}, n={len(out)}, time={elapsed:.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)
    return out
```
